| run-length-tail | (this repo) What the initial odd-run length `r(n) = v2(n+1)` does and does not control: it bounds the descent time from below (`sigma > (19/12) r`, within 0.1% of the structural ceiling `log2 3`) but not from above (no tail `s(r)` works at any `j >= 2`; `r = 1` forces `sigma = 2`).  Answers the open question in `PROGRAM_STATUS`. | proved | `Collatz/Strategy/MersenneDescent.lean`, `MersenneLower.lean`, `RunLengthTail.lean`, `RunLengthUnbounded.lean`, `RunLengthAny.lean`, `LiftExponentThree.lean` |
